The increase plan sparked outrage and concerns from labour unions to business owners.[^cite_note-\:15-239][^cite_note-240] Social media users campaigned to stop paying taxes as a form of protest against the government's policy while labour unions threatened a massive strike if the increase goes through.[^cite_note-\:15-239][^cite_note-241] The [Indonesian Democratic Party of Struggle](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Indonesian_Democratic_Party_of_Struggle) also joined in protest against the proposed raise but was met with criticism from pro-government legislators for being hypocritical as PDI-P once endorsed the idea during Jokowi's presidency by passing the law that will gradually raise it to 12% back in 2021.[^cite_note-242][^cite_note-243] On 31 December 2024, Prabowo and [Minister of Finance](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Minister_of_Finance_\(Indonesia\)) [Sri Mulyani](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sri_Mulyani) announced that the raise to 12% will only be applied to luxurious goods and services such as yachts, private jets and luxurious housing while there will be no tax raise for other goods and services that is not considered as luxury.[^cite_note-244]

#### Budget

During a cabinet meeting on 22 January 2025, Prabowo remarked that he is the first president to oversee a budget that is "up to the ninth unit" and announced that he will cut non-essential spending, from cutting budget for official trips by the ministry to banning ceremonial events that is considered as waste of budget.[^cite_note-245] Doing so, Prabowo managed to cut the budget up to Rp. 306,6 trillion through [Presidential Instruction Number 1 of 2025](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Presidential_Instruction_Number_1_of_2025), and the slashed funds will be relocated towards policies that will benefit the people, such as the free school lunch ([Indonesian](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Indonesian_language): *Makan Bergizi Gratis* (MBG), literally: Free Nutritious Lunch) program.[^cite_note-246]

In September 2025, the central government announced that fiscal transfers to regional governments would be reduced from Rp 920 trillion (USD 55.4 billion) to Rp 650 trillion (USD 39.1 billion) for the 2026 fiscal year.[^cite_note-247]

#### Danantara Sovereign Wealth Fund

On Monday 24 February 2025, Prabowo officially launched the Daya Anagata Nusantara ([Danantara](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Danantara)) [sovereign wealth fund](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sovereign_wealth_fund). During the same launching speech, he also stated that the [Government](77833121.md) has proven its commitment with discipline and accountable financial governance.[^cite_note-248] Danantara works by gathering state-owned enterprise assets to seek funding. These assets will then be used as collateral for debt or even sold, which is estimated to manage up to US\$900 billion in [assets under management](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Assets_under_management). The initial funding is targeted at US\$20 billion.[^cite_note-249]

#### Soup Kitchens

Prabowo oversaw the construction of 12,000 soup kitchens. However, in 2026, the operation and construction of these kitchens were halted as a cost-cutting measure during the June and July holiday period. The public viewed the suspension as abrupt, given the substantial resources invested in the program [^cite_note-250].

### Defence

During the 2024 election, Prabowo proposed several policies in regards to the defence budget, defence industry, strengthening the Reserve Component, increased TNI presence in the border, synergy within defence institutions against separatism, and increased competency in defence technology and professionalism.[^cite_note-251] His policies enacted as Minister of Defence is then continued by Minister [Sjafrie Sjamsoeddin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sjafrie_Sjamsoeddin).[^cite_note-252] Prabowo's modernisation programs on the armed forces from his days as Minister of Defence largely continued under his presidency.

#### Defence acquisitions

Prabowo has stated his interest to join [TAI TF Kaan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/TAI_TF_Kaan) project in a meeting with [Turkish president](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Turkish_president) [Recep Tayyip Erdoğan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Recep_Tayyip_Erdo%C4%9Fan) during his state visit in [Turkey](11125639.md).[^cite_note-253] From the bilateral visit, the two countries agreed to set up joint ventures between strategic defence companies from both countries, aiming to contribute in Indonesia's development of [fifth-generation fighters](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fifth-generation_fighter) and [submarine](28825.md) shipbuilding.[^cite_note-254]

During the bilateral meeting with [French president](24899.md) [Emmanuel Macron](43671127.md), Prabowo and Macron witnessed as their respective defence ministers [Sjafrie Sjamsoeddin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sjafrie_Sjamsoeddin) and [Sébastien Lecornu](54364868.md) signed a [letter of intent](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Letter_of_intent) for a few military hardware, which includes additional [CAESAR self-propelled howitzer](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/CAESAR_self-propelled_howitzer), [Scorpène-class submarines](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Scorp%C3%A8ne-class_submarine), light frigates and finally an additional order of 12 [Dassault Rafale](33731380.md) following up the 42 Rafales ordered when Prabowo was defence minister.[^cite_note-255] The purchase of additional Rafales became noteworthy considering rumours spread that Indonesia may ditch the entire order and replace it with [Chengdu J-10](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chengdu_J-10) of the same quantity after [Pakistan Air Force](377433.md) has shot down one of [Indian Air Force](403355.md) Rafales during [Operation Sindhoor](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Operation_Sindhoor).[^cite_note-256][^cite_note-257] Minister Lecornu in [Twitter](9988187.md) hailed the agreement as a sign of Indonesia's trust on the French defence industry.[^cite_note-258]

In March 2025, it was stated that the [Indonesian Navy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Indonesian_Navy) was interested in acquiring the [Giuseppe Garibaldi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Giuseppe_Garibaldi) aircraft carrier from Italy, along with her airwing of [Harrier II](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/McDonnell_Douglas_AV-8B_Harrier_II) jets.[^cite_note-259] Fincantieri presented a proposal to the Indonesian Navy in July 2025 to convert *Giuseppe Garibaldi* into a [helicopter](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Helicopter_carrier) and [UAV](76996952.md) assault ship.[^cite_note-260] On 13 February 2026, the Indonesian Ministry of Defense announced that Italy had agreed in principle to transfer the Giuseppe Garibaldi to Indonesia as a grant, making it the country's first aircraft carrier. Although the ship itself would be provided free of charge, Indonesia committed to funding its retrofit and technological modifications to meet Indonesian Navy operational requirements. Admiral Muhammad Ali stated that the vessel was expected to arrive before the anniversary of the Indonesian National Armed Forces on 5 October 2026, subject to the completion of negotiations and administrative procedures.[^cite_note-261] According to the Ministry of Defense, the ship will be employed primarily for military operations other than war, particularly humanitarian assistance and disaster relief.[^cite_note-262]

#### Armed Forces Act reform

Under Prabowo, the government has controversially made efforts to revise the [Indonesian National Armed Forces Act](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Indonesian_National_Armed_Forces_Act). The revision has become controversial because some clause of the Act contains potential to revive *[dwifungsi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dwifungsi)* (double role) and allow active [Indonesian National Armed Forces](14650.md) service members to take on civilian roles in government. The lack of transparency, accountability, public participation and the perceived mutilation of the spirit of [Reformasi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Reformasi_\(Indonesia\)) has led to [widespread protest](79252449.md#March).[^cite_note-263] The decision to discuss such revision in [Fairmont Jakarta](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fairmont_Jakarta?action=edit\&redlink=1), a 5-star hotel nearby the [MPR/DPR/DPD building](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/MPR/DPR/DPD_building) instead of the parliament building itself had already sparked criticisms from human rights organisation.[^cite_note-264]

### Foreign policy

See also: [List of international presidential trips made by Prabowo Subianto](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_international_presidential_trips_made_by_Prabowo_Subianto)



Subianto at the group photo of [BRICS](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/BRICS) heads of state at the [18th BRICS summit](82104946.md) in New Delhi, India, 13 September 2026

Prabowo continued the foreign policy of "thousand friends and zero enemies" enacted by his predecessors [Susilo Bambang Yudhoyono](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Susilo_Bambang_Yudhoyono) and [Joko Widodo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Joko_Widodo) with a slightly more active role.[^cite_note-265] In early 2025, Indonesia officially became a member of [BRICS](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/BRICS). Indonesia's membership in BRICS will strengthen Indonesia's position in the global political arena. In a press statement, the Indonesian [Ministry of Foreign Affairs](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ministry_of_Foreign_Affairs_\(Indonesia\)) viewed BRICS membership as a strategic step to enhance collaboration and cooperation with other developing countries, based on the principles of equality, mutual respect, and [sustainable development](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sustainable_development).[^cite_note-266]

Under his administration, he attempted to widen the definition of Indonesia's "free and active" foreign policy through political and economic pragmatism as well as regional stability. He modelled his foreign policy from the words of [Mohammad Hatta](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mohammad_Hatta) as "sailing between two reefs."[^cite_note-267] Compared to Jokowi, Prabowo is seen as a much more aggressive, aspirational, and decisive diplomat.[^cite_note-268] Former President Yudhoyono remarked that Prabowo will be a "foreign policy president."[^cite_note-\:16-269] He was the first [president-elect](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/-elect) in Indonesia's history to conduct foreign visits prior to his inauguration, visiting 20 countries.[^cite_note-\:16-269]

Prabowo is the president with the highest number of foreign trips in Indonesian history, visiting 35 countries[^cite_note-270] and traveled abroad each month except for February and March in 2025; he was out of the country for 95 days between November 2024 to April 2026.[^cite_note-271] He defended his numerous international visits, stating that they are essential for securing Indonesia's oil supply amid the [2026 Iran war](82533582.md) and claiming that these trips have yielded positive outcomes for the country.[^cite_note-272][^cite_note-273]

#### United States



Prabowo meeting with U.S. President [Joe Biden](145422.md) in November 2024

Several months after his inauguration, President Prabowo visited the United States in November 2024. Then, in February 2026, President Prabowo made another overseas visit to the United States to meet with President [Trump](4848272.md). During the visit, President Prabowo had several important agendas, including attending the [Board of Peace](81274522.md) consultation meeting and trade negotiations with the US. President Prabowo sought to highlight Indonesia's multilateral and bilateral cooperation during the visit. He also met with all former Indonesian foreign ministers to discuss strategic plans for negotiations with the US. He had prepared well in advance for these negotiations.[^cite_note-274] During the negotiations, the two countries agreed to renew trade cooperation between Indonesia and the US. Although several points tended to favour the US, Indonesia sought to avoid giving a blank check by requesting tariff adjustments for several of Indonesia's leading products in the US market.[^cite_note-275] In the multilateral negotiations, he also succeeded in building consensus on Indonesia's role and obligations on the Board of Peace, appointing Indonesia as Deputy Commander, although some analysts predicted that the Board of Peace would only serve to legitimise US and Israeli interests in [Gaza](12047.md), as the Board of Peace is not an official [UN peacekeeping force](15652027.md).[^cite_note-276]

#### China



Prabowo and Chinese President [Xi Jinping](2017814.md) in Beijing, 9 November 2024

During a November 2024 presidential visit by Prabowo to China, Indonesia and China signed a [memorandum of understanding](1018279.md) for "joint maritime development" in the area of the two countries "overlapping claims" near the [Natuna Islands](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Natuna_Islands).[^cite_note-\:42-277] Indonesia's Foreign Ministry subsequently issued a statement that the memorandum did not impact Indonesia's sovereignty or rights in the area and stating that in Indonesia's view the Chinese claims do not have a legal basis.[^cite_note-\:42-277] Critics of the memorandum who were quoted by the [South China Morning Post](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/South_China_Morning_Post) contended that the wording could support China's position regarding the South China Sea claims.[^cite_note-\:42-277]

#### Middle East

Further information: [2026 attack on UNIFIL post in Aadchit al-Qusayr](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2026_attack_on_UNIFIL_post_in_Aadchit_al-Qusayr)

Prabowo has continued to vocally advocating for Palestinian independence. In his diplomatic visit to the United States, he proposed the [two-state solution](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Two-state_solution) to US President [Joe Biden](145422.md) to end the [Gaza conflict](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gaza_conflict).[^cite_note-278] He also once again voiced his support in the [D-8 summit](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/D-8_Organization_for_Economic_Cooperation) held in [Cairo](6293.md) while expressing his disappointment in front of other Muslim leaders on the lack of unity, citing [Libya](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Libyan_crisis_\(2011%E2%80%93present\)), [Yemen](46215853.md), and [Sudan](73556699.md).[^cite_note-279][^cite_note-280][^cite_note-281] His disappointment gained support from Malaysian Prime Minister [Anwar Ibrahim](19264815.md), calling his disappointment "a bitter truth."[^cite_note-282]



Subianto with Saudi Crown Prince [Mohammed bin Salman](38689358.md) at the [Royal State Palace, Jeddah](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Royal_State_Palace,_Jeddah), Saudi Arabia, 2 July 2025

When it was Prabowo's turn to speak in the D-8 summit, some dignitaries, including Turkish President [Recep Tayyip Erdoğan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Recep_Tayyip_Erdo%C4%9Fan), "walked out" from the summit, nudging Prabowo's seat on his way out.[^cite_note-283] Many at home perceived this diplomatic gaffe as Prabowo being "too preachy" and indirectly offending Erdogan, whose country enjoys [NATO](21133.md) support and having diplomatic ties with [Israel](9282173.md), briefly straining relations between both countries.[^cite_note-284] However, Cabinet Secretary [Teddy Indra Wijaya](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Teddy_Indra_Wijaya) clarified that Erdogan had already apologised before the conference session started and had requested Prabowo to switch turns with him so that he could leave early to attend a bilateral meeting with another leader.[^cite_note-285] The Turkish Embassy in Indonesia states that relations between Indonesia and Turkey will remain harmonious despite Erdogan's leave.[^cite_note-286]

Prabowo once again advocated for a [two-state solution](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Two-state_solution) to end the long ongoing conflict in [Gaza](74998519.md) in front of [French president](24899.md) [Emmanuel Macron](43671127.md),[^cite_note-287] who some time before meeting Prabowo has announced plans for French recognition of Palestine.[^cite_note-288] He also said that he is open to [normalise relations with Israel](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Indonesia%E2%80%93Israel_relations) once [Palestinian independence](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Palestinian_independence) is acknowledged.[^cite_note-289] In his statement, Palestine's independence is the only solution for a long lasting peace in the [Middle East](19323.md) while stating that [Israel](9282173.md) also has an inalienable right to exist as a [sovereign state](23604120.md) and Indonesia will recognise Israel only after Palestine is fully independent.[^cite_note-290] Despite the idea of recognising Israel after Palestine became independent became controversial, the [Indonesian Ulema Council](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Indonesian_Ulema_Council) (MUI) endorsed the idea, reminding the public that his statement is not a pro-Israel statement.[^cite_note-\:18-291] However, MUI told that Prabowo must hold Israel accountable for their [war crimes in Gaza](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Israeli_war_crimes_in_the_Gaza_war) even if he wishes to normalise ties with Israel.[^cite_note-\:18-291] The [Prosperous Justice Party](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Prosperous_Justice_Party) and the [United Development Party](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Development_Party) also gave their support to Prabowo's statement but told Prabowo to focus more on Palestinian independence first,[^cite_note-292] while [PDI-P](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/PDI-P) politician [Djarot Saiful Hidayat](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Djarot_Saiful_Hidayat) state that Prabowo should not rush to recognise Israel as a state, as it is considered a violation to the [Constitution of Indonesia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Constitution_of_Indonesia).[^cite_note-293]



Prabowo at the [Gaza peace summit](81324529.md) in [Sharm El Sheikh](419335.md), Egypt, 13 October 2025

During the [Eightieth session of the United Nations General Assembly](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Eightieth_session_of_the_United_Nations_General_Assembly), Prabowo reiterated his stance on recognising Israel with the condition that Israel must recognise Palestine as an independent nation.[^cite_note-294] While praising [France](5843419.md), [United Kingdom](31717.md), [Portugal](23033.md) and other countries for their recognition of Palestine, Prabowo also pressured those who had yet to recognise Palestine to do so as soon as possible to stop the humanitarian crisis there.[^cite_note-295] Once again, it sparked debate in social media with arguments for the two state solution said Prabowo's speech has placed one step closer to peace while arguments against it said that Israel's atrocities has made the two state solution no longer viable and Prabowo had violated the [Constitution of Indonesia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Constitution_of_Indonesia) for saying in favour of Israeli recognition.[^cite_note-296][^cite_note-297] His speech generated even more controversy in social media after [Israeli prime minister](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Israeli_prime_minister) [Benjamin Netanyahu](56472.md) praised Prabowo's speech.[^cite_note-298] He was nevertheless applauded domestically as Speaker [Puan Maharani](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Puan_Maharani) states her proudness as she considers Prabowo's speech as reflecting Indonesia's commitments to end the humanity crisis in Palestine,[^cite_note-299] while former president [Joko Widodo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Joko_Widodo) states that Prabowo's speech is a firm and brave message for peace.[^cite_note-300] Internationally, [US President](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/US_President) [Donald Trump](4848272.md) was impressed especially when Prabowo pounded the podium as he spoke while French president [Emmanuel Macron](43671127.md) thanked Prabowo for his "strong" statements and told him that other countries were taking notes of his commitment.[^cite_note-301] Prabowo later told the media that his speech was positively received and he got words of appreciation from numerous state leaders for "truly want to find a middle path", in which he hopes to find a substantial and just way to end the conflict.[^cite_note-302] He later attended the [Gaza Peace Summit](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gaza_Peace_Summit) in [Sharm El Sheikh](419335.md) as the sole representative from Southeast Asia, witnessing the signings of the accords and offering Indonesia's support for Gaza's reconstruction while advocating for the two-state solution.[^cite_note-303] In his speech at the [2025 United Nations General Assembly](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Eightieth_session_of_the_United_Nations_General_Assembly), Prabowo remarked that Indonesia would be willing to provide up "20,000 or even more" peacekeeping personnel in case an UN mission to Gaza was authorised.[^cite_note-304]

#### India



Prabowo with Indian Prime Minister [Narendra Modi](444222.md) and President [Droupadi Murmu](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Droupadi_Murmu) as chief guest of the [Republic Day](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Republic_Day_\(India\)) celebration, 2025

Prabowo was invited by Prime Minister [Narendra Modi](444222.md)'s government as chief guest of the [Republic Day](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Republic_Day_\(India\)) celebration in 2025. The visit is to further strengthen the strategic partnership between the two countries. The Indonesian president was welcomed by Minister of State for External Affairs [Pabitra Margherita](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pabitra_Margherita). The trade between the two countries in 2023 stands at USD 29.40 billion.[^cite_note-305] Prabowo was met by the Indian Foreign Minister [S. Jaishankar](40435868.md).[^cite_note-306] The Indian Ministry of External Affairs described the relationship as "warm and friendly… spanning over millennia." President Prabowo was given a ceremonial welcome by the President of India [Droupadi Murmu](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Droupadi_Murmu) and Prime Minister Narendra Modi.[^cite_note-307] Prabowo said Indonesia considers India a great friend and the first country that recognised Indonesian independence.[^cite_note-308] A 352-member Indonesian military marching contingent participated in the [Republic Day parade](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Delhi_Republic_Day_parade) in New Delhi. During the visit the two sides are to discuss and conclude deals in trade, healthcare, digital technologies, energy and tourism, as well as the purchase of the [BrahMos](1814876.md) supersonic cruise missile.[^cite_note-309]

Prior to his state visit to India in 2025, Prabowo claimed to have a genome test on his ancestry and the test reported him as partially Indian. In front of the Indian government, Prabowo admitted that he danced to [Bollywood](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bollywood) songs to which he attributed this habit to his DNA test result.[^cite_note-310] He again raised the matter when [Narendra Modi](444222.md) visited Indonesia on 2026 and states that he cannot help himself dancing to Indian music despite in a formal setting between both leaders to which he also jokes that his ministers and generals may also have Indian DNA due to how well they sang Indian songs.[^cite_note-311][^cite_note-312]

#### Russia



Prabowo and Russian President [Vladimir Putin](32817.md) in St. Petersburg, Russia, 19 June 2025

On 19 June 2025, Prabowo attended the [St. Petersburg International Economic Forum](26267797.md) as a guest of honour. He met Russian President [Vladimir Putin](32817.md) and the two heads of state signed a strategic partnership agreement. In addition, the Indonesian and Russian sovereign funds [Danantara](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Danantara) and the [Russian Direct Investment Fund](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Russian_Direct_Investment_Fund) signed an agreement to create an investment fund worth [€](9472.md)2 billion (US\$2.9 billion).[^cite_note-313][^cite_note-\:19-314] His visit to Russia coincides with the [51st G7 summit](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/51st_G7_summit) at [Kananaskis](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kananaskis_Improvement_District), in which Prabowo was also invited to attend but didn't.[^cite_note-\:19-314] Despite the apparent snub cited by Western aligned media,[^cite_note-315][^cite_note-\:19-314] Prabowo clarified his reason to skip the summit is because Indonesia's non-bloc stance and commitment issues made prior to the G7 invitation.[^cite_note-316][^cite_note-317]

### Social

#### Free school meals

Main article: [Free Nutritious Meals](82783358.md)

His campaign team introduced a goal of a free school school lunch program by distributing free milk and lunch meals.[^cite_note-318] After Prabowo was declared as president-elect, pilot program of the policy was enacted. Prabowo said that he wanted to enact this because he saw nearly 25% of Indonesian children skip their breakfast and he wanted to know if the program could succeed by conducting pilot programs.[^cite_note-319] On 24 May 2024, he changed the program into free nutritious meal program. Overall, this program is designed with the aim of building superior resources, reducing stunting and poverty rates while driving the economy.[^cite_note-320] This program is also one of Prabowo's key policies in realising the [Golden Indonesia 2045 Vision](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Golden_Indonesia_2045_Vision) that targets the creation of a golden generation by utilising [demographic dividends](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Demographic_dividend) that can launch Indonesia into a [developed country](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Developed_country).[^cite_note-321]

The program was officially rolled out since 6 January 2025 in 26 provinces of Indonesia, carried out by the [National Nutrition Agency](77656859.md), and targeting children from [preschool](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Preschool) to [secondary school](554992.md) students, as well as pregnant and nursing mothers with the aim of able to bring benefit to at least 82,9 million beneficiaries.[^cite_note-\:02-322] However, the effectiveness of this policy was repeatedly questioned. With a huge budget needed to execute the program, the program may risk cutting the budget of other social programs and caused economic instability.[^cite_note-\:03-323] In addition, the program has been marked by repeated cases of [food poisoning](531611.md); by 15 August 2025, over 1,000 people had been sickened.[^cite_note-324]

#### People's School

Prabowo established *Sekolah Rakyat*, an educational initiative with the aims to break free from poverty through [boarding school](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Boarding_school) education. The school will be free of charge and ensure that poor families are given equal educational opportunities under a stable and conducive learning environment.[^cite_note-325] The program was launched on July 2025 with poor and extremely poor families as the main recipients of the program with the government has designated 53 locations across East Java, Papua, Kalimantan and East Nusa Tenggara and aimed to improve human resources quality.

#### Health

On 10 February 2025, Prabowo's government enacted a free health check service with aims to improve the [quality of life](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Quality_of_life_\(healthcare\)) of the population through the use of SatuSehat app launched by the [Ministry of Health](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ministry_of_Health_\(Indonesia\)).[^cite_note-326] The government aimed to decrease [mortality rate](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mortality_rate) caused by disease that can be avoided through [preventive healthcare](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Preventive_healthcare) such as [cardiovascular disease](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cardiovascular_disease), [stroke](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Stroke), [diabetes](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Diabetes) and [tuberculosis](30653.md).[^cite_note-327][^cite_note-328]

### New capital

Construction of [Nusantara](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nusantara_\(city\)) under Prabowo's presidency continued despite no longer be considered as priority due to reallocation of other policies such as the [free meal program](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Free_Meal_Program_\(Indonesia\)).[^cite_note-329]

On 21 September 2025, Prabowo signed a presidential decree that officially made Nusantara as the political [capital city](181337.md) of Indonesia and targeted for its realisation to be implemented in 2028.[^cite_note-330] However, this decree was met with suspicion as the decree only mandated for the [House of Representatives](737841.md) and [Regional Representative Council](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Regional_Representative_Council) to move to Nusantara,[^cite_note-331] while analysts criticise the lack of seriousness to relocate away from [Jakarta](16275.md).[^cite_note-332]

## Political views

|  | This section needs to be **updated**. Please help update this article to reflect recent events or newly available information. *(September 2026)* |
| --- | --- |

Prabowo Subianto said in his November 2023 speech at the Institute for Development of Economics and Finance (INDEF) think thank that Indonesia needs a middle way between [capitalism](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Capitalism) and [socialism](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Socialism), which would be a [Pancasila](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pancasila) [economy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Economy) how the third president of Indonesia [B.J. Habibie](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/B.J._Habibie) wanted.[^cite_note-333][^cite_note-334] Besides his own variant of a [Third Way](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Third_Way), he advocates for [anti-neoliberalism](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Anti-neoliberalism).[^cite_note-335] In foreign policy, Prabowo has expressed a desire for closer diplomatic ties with [China](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/China%E2%80%93Indonesia_relations) and [India](19319107.md), while calling for distance from the [West](21208200.md).[^cite_note-336] In June 2023, during his tenure as defence minister, Prabowo proposed a peace plan to end the [Russo-Ukrainian War](42085878.md), involving holding referendums in disputed areas between the two countries, which was subsequently condemned by Ukraine for appearing pro-Russia.[^cite_note-337] Then-president Jokowi later clarified that Prabowo's suggestion was his personal viewpoint and did not reflect the official position of the Indonesian government.[^cite_note-338] In January 2022, Prabowo expressed openness to the normalisation of [Indonesia–Israel relations](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Indonesia%E2%80%93Israel_relations).[^cite_note-339]

## Controversies

|  | This ["criticism" or "controversy" section](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia\:Criticism#Avoid_sections_and_articles_focusing_on_criticisms_or_controversies) **may compromise the article's [neutrality](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia\:Neutral_point_of_view#Article_structure)**. Please help [integrate negative information](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia\:Structure) into other sections or remove [undue focus on minor criticisms and controversies](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia\:BALASP). *(April 2025)* |
| --- | --- |

### Internal army dispute

In 1983, according to [Sintong Panjaitan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sintong_Panjaitan), Prabowo had a dispute with a few generals who plotted to overthrow [Suharto](217513.md) to the point that he plotted to use [Detachment 81](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kopassus) to attempt kidnapping these generals, including General [L. B. Moerdani](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/L._B._Moerdani) who was rumoured to be plotting a [coup](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Coup) against Suharto. However, his plot was stopped by [Luhut Binsar Pandjaitan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Luhut_Binsar_Pandjaitan), the commander of Detachment 81. Prabowo was his deputy commander at that time.[^cite_note-340] Major Luhut Pandjaitan ordered to cancel the attempt and confiscate all weapons and equipment. The threat of a coup against Suharto was later found to be false and Prabowo was given a leave due to stress.[^cite_note-merdeka1-341] However, Prabowo denied Sintong Panjaitan's version of the story by laughing. According to him, it was unreasonable that a captain could lead his troops to fight and kidnap a general. He calmly said that every time there was a new book, there would be a new coup accusation levelled against himself, and left everyone with their own version of the story.[^cite_note-merdeka1-341]

Prabowo was once again involved in a dispute with Moerdani ahead of the [1988](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/1987_Indonesian_legislative_election) MPR General Assembly. According to [Kivlan Zen](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kivlan_Zen), rumours persisted that Moerdani would use his office of [commander of the Indonesian National Armed Forces](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Commander_of_the_Indonesian_National_Armed_Forces) to gain support from the ABRI faction to run as vice president to [Suharto](217513.md). Prabowo reported this to Suharto and Suharto heeded Prabowo's report by replacing Moerdani with [Try Sutrisno](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Try_Sutrisno) as commander of the armed forces. The position of vice president then fell to [Sudharmono](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sudharmono).[^cite_note-merdeka1-341] Moerdani's dismissal sparked worries of a coup, however the coup never really happened. Despite this, it showed Prabowo's rising influence in the armed forces and on Suharto's decision making.[^cite_note-merdeka1-341]

### Occupation of East Timor

In 1983, it was claimed that Prabowo commanded the Indonesian special forces responsible for the [Kraras massacres](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kraras_massacre) in East Timor.[^cite_note-Santoso-33] The survivors of these massacres were locked up in a concentration camp guarded by Prabowo's men, where many died of starvation and ill-treatment.[^cite_note-\:17-34] Prabowo claimed he was nowhere near the Viqueque district when the massacre happened, and neither the UN nor the government of Timor Leste have ever proffered charges of human rights violations against him.[^cite_note-pbw-35] José Manuel Tesoro, writing for *[Asiaweek](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Asiaweek)* in 2000, contacted four separate non-governmental organisations including [Tapol](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tapol) in London; Solidamor in Jakarta; the HAK Foundation, headquartered in [Dili](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dili); and the East Timor Action Network (ETAN) in New York, but they could not provide any eyewitness reports, transcripts of intercepted communications, leaked papers, or anything that could substantiate Prabowo's involvement.[^cite_note-jmt-36][^cite_note-Santoso-33]

### Kidnapping of activists

Between 1997 and 1998, Kopassus members from Team Mawar (Rose Team) were responsible for [pro-democracy activists kidnappings](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/1997%E2%80%9398_activists_kidnappings_in_Indonesia) of at least 22 people mainly in Jakarta.[^cite_note-JP2020-342] Nine activists were released and 13 remain missing.[^cite_note-JP2020-342][^cite_note-343] In 1999, 11 members of Tim Mawar were found guilty by a military court.[^cite_note-JP2020-342] However, they appealed the verdict to the Supreme Court, which was not made public and only revealed in 2007, and were never jailed and all but one remained in the military.[^cite_note-JP2020-342][^cite_note-HRW2018-344][^cite_note-345] In September 2020, Defence Minister Prabowo appointed two of the Team Mawar officers, two serving [brigadier generals](206220.md), to senior positions in the [Ministry of Defence](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ministry_of_Defense_\(Indonesia\)) following approval by President [Joko Widodo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Joko_Widodo).[^cite_note-HRW2018-344] [Amnesty International](18947898.md) criticised the appointments as the President and the [DPR](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/People's_Representative_Council) had promised to investigate missing activist cases and instead placed suspects in positions of power.[^cite_note-JP2020-342][^cite_note-346] and shortly after Prabowo was inaugurated as president two more members of the Team Mawar became heads of the [National Cyber and Crypto Agency](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/National_Cyber_and_Crypto_Agency) and the Main Secretariat of the [State Intelligence Agency](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/State_Intelligence_Agency_\(Indonesia\)).[^cite_note-347]

### Paradise Papers

See also: [Paradise Papers](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Paradise_Papers)

In November 2017, an investigation conducted by the [International Consortium of Investigative Journalism](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/International_Consortium_of_Investigative_Journalists) cited his name in [the list of politicians named in](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_people_and_organisations_named_in_the_Paradise_Papers) "[Paradise Papers](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Paradise_Papers)" allegations.[^cite_note-348]

### Mirage 2000 purchase

On 13 February 2024, the [Corruption Eradication Commission](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Corruption_Eradication_Commission) had received a report of alleged corruption conducted by Prabowo in the purchase of ex-[Qatar Emiri Air Force](4014890.md) [Dassault Mirage 2000-5](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dassault_Mirage_2000) jet aircraft which was cancelled by the Ministry of Defence due to fiscal limitations.[^cite_note-\:9-349][^cite_note-350] Allegedly, the purchased aircraft will be bought at a much higher price and there are gratification involved.[^cite_note-\:9-349][^cite_note-\:10-351] Deputy Minister of Defence [Muhammad Herindra](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Muhammad_Herindra) denied the allegations, dismissing the rumours as slanderous allegations and fake news.[^cite_note-\:10-351] In response, the Ministry of Defence appointed lawyer [Hotman Paris Hutapea](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hotman_Paris_Hutapea) to lead the defence.[^cite_note-352] Despite being mentioned in a report that allegedly uncovered the corruption, [Group of States against Corruption](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Group_of_States_against_Corruption) (GRECO) stated it had "never have done any work related to Indonesia."[^cite_note-353]

### Political gaffes

On 18 September 2017, at the launch of a book on his father's political economy theory, Prabowo made a speech warning Indonesia could break apart in 2030. "In other countries, they have made studies, where the Republic of Indonesia has been declared no more in 2030," he said. A video clip of the speech was posted to Gerindra's official Facebook page on 18 March 2018. When asked which studies Prabowo was referring to, Gerindra official M. Husein Mohi said, "Prabowo Subianto has read various writings of people that are outside the country, intellectual observers that exist. You can also see them online."[^cite_note-354] It was subsequently revealed the "studies" were actually a 2015 science-fiction war novel called *[Ghost Fleet](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ghost_Fleet_\(novel\))* by US authors August Cole and [P. W. Singer](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/P._W._Singer). A note by the authors at the start of the book states: "The following was inspired by real-world trends and technologies. But, ultimately, it is a work of fiction, not prediction."[^cite_note-355] Bemused by Prabowo citing the book, Singer posted on Twitter: "Indonesian opposition leader cites #GhostFleet in fiery campaign speeches... There have been many unexpected twists and turns from this book experience, but this may take the cake."[^cite_note-356]

In November 2018, he was embroiled in controversy for a part of his campaign in Boyolali mentioning [the appearance of people](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lookism) in [Boyolali](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Boyolali_Regency), [Central Java](453584.md). After mentioning names of various luxury hotels in Jakarta whose name he claimed the rally can never pronounce, he said, "..but I am sure you have not entered these hotels. If you want to enter them you might be kicked out. You don't look like wealthy people, you have a Boyolali look." This has led to several Boyolali polling stations for 2019 election not voting him at all. Prabowo lost the regency again in 2024 election in spite of overall win in Central Java province.[^cite_note-357][^cite_note-358] The next month, Prabowo was ridiculed after erroneously stating that [Haiti](13373.md), a republic in the [Caribbean](18956035.md), is an [African](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/African_countries) country. In a speech made on 23 December 2018 in [Solo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Surakarta), Central Java, Prabowo said the Indonesian government had driven part of Indonesia's wealth offshore. "If this continues to go on, Indonesia will continue to be impoverished," he said. "We, Indonesians, are on par with African impoverished countries such as [Rwanda](25645.md), Haiti, and small islands like [Kiribati](16674.md), which we don't even know where it's located," he added.[^cite_note-359] On 17 January 2019, in the first debate between the candidates in Indonesia's April 2019 presidential election, Prabowo said some Indonesian governors deserve higher salaries considering the size of their provinces. He gave the example of Central Java province, which he claimed is larger than [Malaysia](3607937.md). Local media reports pointed out that Central Java is 32,544.12 square kilometres, while Malaysia is 330,323 square kilometres.[^cite_note-360] After the media reported on the error, Prabowo's campaign team claimed he had actually been referring to the population totals of Malaysia and Central Java.[^cite_note-361] In the same debate, Prabowo claimed that terrorist attacks in Indonesia were caused by poverty and perpetrated by non-Muslims disguised as Muslims, sent by other countries and controlled by foreigners. Media reports refuted his claims, pointing out that some Indonesian terrorist bombers were not poor and were not manipulated by foreigners.[^cite_note-362]

On 12 December 2023, Prabowo went viral after his commentary about his rival [Anies Baswedan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Anies_Baswedan) on ethics by saying "*Ndasmu etik!*" (English: God damn ethics!) which is considered insulting him in an internal party congress held by Gerindra. His spokesperson later stated that it was just a joke.[^cite_note-\:2-363] *Ndasmu* would appear again in Gerindra's 17th anniversary party in Bogor where Prabowo criticizes his critics over the perception that his government was a puppet government of [Joko Widodo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Joko_Widodo).[^cite_note-\:22-364]

During the anniversary of [National Awakening Party](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/National_Awakening_Party) on 24 July 2026, Prabowo said that there are still "*[londo ireng](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Belanda_Hitam)*" to criticize perceived enemies of the state to which he describes them as masquerading as journalists, experts and NGOs who only seek to report on the bad results of his government policies. He said that those *londo irengs* still exists, just changing their clothes.[^cite_note-365] The Association of Indonesian Journalists (AJI) and several other NGOs demanded his apology and criticizes his speech as discrediting their profession, endangering free speech and being tone-deaf to current issues.[^cite_note-366] [Hasan Nasbi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hasan_Nasbi), special advisor to the president, states that Prabowo's speech was meant to criticize troublemakers and he slammed those who demand the apology for stirring up unnecessary chaos.[^cite_note-367]

On 21 September 2026 in the [National Mandate Party](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/National_Mandate_Party) 28th anniversary party, Prabowo gave a speech criticizing experts who criticizes his free nutritious meal program, claiming that these so-called "experts" had spread lies and deceit and among these experts he said that they're, "too intelligent that (he/she) became stupid!" (Indonesian: *Saking pintarnya jadi goblok*).[^cite_note-\:22-364]

### Political endorsement

During the [2024 Central Java gubernatorial election](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2024_Central_Java_gubernatorial_election), Prabowo gave his public endorsement of support to [Ahmad Luthfi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ahmad_Luthfi) and [Taj Yasin Maimoen](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Taj_Yasin_Maimoen) through a video uploaded in Taj Yasin's Instagram account. His endorsement was slammed by the [PDI-P](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Indonesian_Democratic_Party_of_Struggle), and [Deputy Speaker of the People's Consultative Assembly](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Deputy_Speaker_of_the_People's_Consultative_Assembly) [Bambang Wuryanto](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bambang_Wuryanto?action=edit\&redlink=1) called the endorsement as "worrying" and reminded Prabowo that he is the president, not just a party leader.[^cite_note-368] Many has also questioned the ethics behind the endorsement as legal experts believed that the president of Indonesia must not meddle around with legal institutions in favour of a candidate[^cite_note-369] and [General Election Supervisory Agency](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/General_Election_Supervisory_Agency) (Bawaslu) announced that they will investigate the video for any offences.[^cite_note-370] However, Prabowo's endorsement was also defended as [NasDem](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/NasDem_Party) politician [Muhammad Rifqinizamy Karsayuda](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Muhammad_Rifqinizamy_Karsayuda) and Head of [Presidential Communication Office](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Presidential_Communication_Office_\(Indonesia\)) [Hasan Nasbi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hasan_Nasbi) said that Prabowo spoke as a party chairman and there is no restrictions on presidents being a chairman of their own parties[^cite_note-371][^cite_note-372] while [Coordinating Minister for Political and Security Affairs](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Coordinating_Ministry_for_Political_and_Security_Affairs) [Budi Gunawan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Budi_Gunawan) states that Prabowo's endorsement is a "normal matter" in democracies.[^cite_note-373] Bawaslu found that although the video featuring him did show political campaign message, their investigation result shows that he did not commit any offences because the video was posted within the social media campaign period between 25 September to 23 November, and Prabowo is allowed to campaign for his endorsed candidates as long as either he took his time off from the office or he done it on a holiday, which in Taj Yasin's clarification to Bawaslu states that the video was created on Sunday, 3 November 2024.[^cite_note-374]

### Allegations of authoritarianism

As a president elected in 2024 and being [Suharto](217513.md)'s son-in-law, Prabowo's leadership style was criticised for his authoritarian style of rule and allegedly revived the practice of *[Dwifungsi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dwifungsi)*, where active military personnel can concurrently serve in the civilian government while maintaining military command. His background as a military commander rises when *Dwifungsi* was at its peak of power. The [Reformasi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Reformasi_\(Indonesia\)) that has sought to limit the influence of the military in civilian government has its values now threatened by moves to again empower the military, such as the revision of the Law No. 34 Year 2004.[^cite_note-\:52-375]

During his time as defence minister under [Joko Widodo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Joko_Widodo), Prabowo is considered to have restored the role of the military in many aspects of civilian life. He was known to involve the military in government programs such as the free meal program, food estate, and many other roles that is supposed to be done by the civilian sector. Few of his cabinet ministers also have military roles, which further strengthens the perception that the direction of government tends to revive [New Order](2864794.md)-style practices.[^cite_note-\:52-375] His most controversial appointment in his cabinet however is when he appointed [Teddy Indra Wijaya](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Teddy_Indra_Wijaya) who was still active in the military to be his [cabinet secretary](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cabinet_Secretariat_\(Indonesia\)), which generate criticism from academicians and legal experts saying Prabowo has violated the Law No. 34 Year 2004 Article 47 which states that active service members of the [Indonesian National Armed Forces](14650.md) must resign or retire from military life if appointed or given a civilian role.[^cite_note-376][^cite_note-377][^cite_note-378]

## Personal life



Prabowo (centre left) at his wedding to Titiek Suharto, 1983

Prabowo married [Titiek Suharto](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Titiek_Suharto) on 8 May 1983. Titiek is the daughter of Indonesian President Suharto, but the couple separated shortly after Suharto's presidency ended in 1998.[^cite_note-379] Prabowo saw himself as a potential successor to Suharto and remained a strong supporter of the regime.[^cite_note-NYT_Paddock-6] Prabowo and Titiek have one son together, fashion designer [Ragowo Hediprasetyo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Didit_Hediprasetyo) (Didit), who was born in March 1984.[^cite_note-380]

Prabowo is a [cat lover](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cat_fancy) and owns a cat named [Bobby Kertanegara](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bobby_Kertanegara). Bobby was a stray who was adopted by Prabowo from the streets after a chance meeting, and he often showed up in meetings that Prabowo attended in his residence at Kertanegara.[^cite_note-\:3-381] It was revealed later in a small talk with [Deputy Prime Minister](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Deputy_Prime_Minister_of_the_United_Kingdom) [Angela Rayner](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Angela_Rayner) that he has 8 cats.[^cite_note-382] Aside from cats, he also owns 18 horses in his hill estate in Jonggol Mountains, Bojong Koneng, [Bogor Regency](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bogor_Regency) (southeast of [Sentul City](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sentul_City)), and he often invited guests to ride one of his horses, especially when Jokowi and [Gibran](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gibran_Rakabuming_Raka) visited.[^cite_note-383] He had once brought his horse to campaign rallies especially in [Gelora Bung Karno Stadium](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gelora_Bung_Karno_Stadium).[^cite_note-384] Because of his hobby of raising horses, Prabowo is known to support [equestrian sports](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Equestrianism) as the [Equestrian Association of Indonesia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Equestrian_Association_of_Indonesia) once received 28 horses from Prabowo's personal wealth to support the national team in preparation for the [2025 SEA Games](45634454.md) while being given a medal target for [polo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Polo) directly from the president.[^cite_note-385] In a bilateral meeting with Prime Minister [Mark Carney](13571049.md), Prabowo expressed that he dreamed to become a *[Mountie](69080.md)* during his youth.[^cite_note-386]

Prabowo usually wears [khaki](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Khaki) clothes with many pockets. He himself stated that he liked this suit for practical reasons. Prabowo started wearing it frequently when he was paired with Megawati in the 2009 presidential election.[^cite_note-387]

In addition to [Indonesian](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Indonesian_language), Prabowo speaks the [Banyumasan dialect](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Banyumasan_dialect) of [Javanese](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javanese_language), his father's mother tongue,[^cite_note-388] and is fluent in [English](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/English_language), [Dutch](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dutch_language), [French](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/French_language), and [German](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/German_language). He also has limited proficiency in [Arabic](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arabic), which he learned during his time in Jordan.[^cite_note-\:14-389]

### Religion

In an interview in 2018, Prabowo stated that he was a [Muslim](19541.md) who adhered to nationalist views.[^cite_note-\:11-390] He added that he had insufficient knowledge about [Islam](6037917.md).[^cite_note-\:11-390] Prabowo has stated that he could not read the [Quran](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Quran) fluently.[^cite_note-391]

Prabowo said that he was not born into a family steeped in Islamic teachings but grew up with his colleagues who were members of various Islamic community organisations.[^cite_note-392]

On 13 May 2023, Prabowo stated that he was proud to adhere to Islam, which he considers to be the pioneer in many [scientific and technological breakthroughs](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Islamic_attitudes_towards_science) around the world.[^cite_note-393] On 19 November, he stated that he felt comfortable with adhering to Islamic beliefs from the teachings of [Nahdlatul Ulama](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nahdlatul_Ulama) (NU) because he believed that the latter advocated for a form of Islam that is tolerant, protective of society, and moderate.[^cite_note-394]

### Health

On 30 June 2024, Prabowo announced that he was undergoing a surgery on his left foot via his Instagram account.[^cite_note-\:13-395] In his post, he explained that he had injured his left foot twice.[^cite_note-\:13-395] He first injured it when he was participating in a [paratrooping](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Paratrooping) exercise in [West Germany](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/West_Germany) while he was training with [GSG 9](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/GSG_9). Additionally, he experienced another foot injury when he fell down from a cliff in a combat zone in the 1980s.[^cite_note-396] Prabowo, who was 30 at the time of the accident, did not experience further issues until his 2024 campaign, when he was seen walking on a limp on his left foot. This prompted rival supporters to mock his physique.[^cite_note-397] Prabowo underwent a successful surgery on his left foot a week later at the National Defence Central Hospital.[^cite_note-398][^cite_note-399]

### Other honours



2024 Ukrainian stamp featuring Prabowo

On 17 June 2009, Prabowo was declared a member of the Lumban Tobing clan of the [Toba Batak people](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Toba_Batak_people). The clan awarding was facilitated by the Punguan Siraja Lumban Tobing Association (PPSLB) and took place at the Lake Toba Convention Center, [Medan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Medan).[^cite_note-421]

On 28 December 2011, Prabowo received the traditional title of Tongkonan from the indigenous people of Siguntu village, [Rantepao](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rantepao), [North Toraja](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/North_Toraja_Regency). The presentation of the traditional title which was accompanied by the Rambu Solo' mourning ceremony was witnessed by the Governor of South Sulawesi [Syahrul Yasin Limpo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Syahrul_Yasin_Limpo), Deputy Governor of South Sulawesi [Agus Arifin Nu'mang](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Agus_Arifin_Nu'mang?action=edit\&redlink=1), Commander of Kodam VII/Wirabuana Major General [Muhammad Nizam](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Muhammad_Nizam?action=edit\&redlink=1), Regent of Tanah Toraja [Theofilus Allorerung](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Theofilus_Allorerung?action=edit\&redlink=1), Regent of North Toraja Frederik Batti Sorring, along with thousands of local residents.[^cite_note-422]

He also received the title of Friend of Indonesian Santri from the Zainul Hasan Genggong Islamic Boarding School, [Probolinggo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Probolinggo), on 2 January 2024.[^cite_note-423]

On 28 February 2024, Prabowo was awarded an honorary four-star general rank by President Joko Widodo.[^cite_note-424]

In November 2024, [Ukrposhta](16794767.md), Ukraine's national postal service, issued a limited-edition stamp honouring Prabowo. The initiative was spearheaded by the Ukrainian Initiative, an [NGO](46539.md) led by Indonesianist Yurii Kosenko, who described the stamp as both a tribute to Prabowo and a symbol of [Indonesia-Ukraine friendship](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Indonesia-Ukraine_relations).[^cite_note-425]
